Model before rewriting:

find a: int(-1..2)
find b: int(-1..2)

find m: matrix indexed by [int(-1..1)] of int(1..2)
find z: bool

such that

(z) <-> (m[sum([a,b;int(1..)])] = c),
m[0] = 1,
m[1] = 1

   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])

new variables:
  find c#sign_magnitude_1: bool

  find c#sign_magnitude_3: bool
new constraints:
  or([!(c#sign_magnitude_2),and([!(c#sign_magnitude_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)])
  !(c#sign_magnitude_3)
  (!(c#sign_magnitude_3)) -> (or([!(c#sign_magnitude_2),and([!(c#sign_magnitude_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)]))
  (c#sign_magnitude_3) -> (or([c#sign_magnitude_1,c#sign_magnitude_2;int(1..)]))
  or([and([SATInt(SignMagnitude, [c#sign_magnitude_1,c#sign_magnitude_2,c#sign_magnitude_3;int(1..)] [1, 2]) >= 1,SATInt(SignMagnitude, [c#sign_magnitude_1,c#sign_magnitude_2,c#sign_magnitude_3;int(1..)] [1, 2]) <= 2;int(1..)]);int(1..)])
